- **T1-CI second (belt-and-suspenders):** Lower `GIVEN_UP_RETRY_LIMIT` from `2` to `1` in `PlannerAgent`. `GoalSimilarityService.countGivenUpMatches()` already uses Monge-Elkan semantic similarity at 0.88, so paraphrased re-injections are already detected — the only gap is the retry count. One prior GIVEN_UP is sufficient evidence of unachievability; the double-attempt only doubles churn. One-line constant change.

---

### Chain C — Isolated in-memory state (T2-BZ, layers A+B done)
**Goal:** Detect and prevent controllers from declaring their own private copies of data owned by another controller.

- Layer A (`IsolatedStateHeuristic` — CRITICAL reject on shadow list) done 2026-04-15.
- Layer B (SHARED_STATE_RULE in PlannerProtocolContract) done 2026-04-13.
- Layer C (broaden T2-BX activation gate) removed — the @RequestMapping noun-extraction approach was too Spring/REST-specific. Needs a general-purpose design.

---

### Chain D — Criteria quality across subgoals (already partially shipped)
**Goal:** Ensure every subgoal carries criteria that enforce both its own requirements AND the root goal's cross-cutting invariants.

```
T2-CA Stage 1 [x done]  →  T2-CA Stage 2 [x done]  →  T2-BY (open)  →  T3-BH (open)
```

- T2-CA Stages 1+2 are shipped. The remaining open work:
- **T2-BY:** split subgoals only when criteria span independent concerns. Prevents multi-endpoint criteria from creating unresolvable evaluation conflicts.
- **T3-BH:** plan rationale and sibling context in prompts — agents see what siblings are doing, preventing re-implementation of already-complete sibling work.


---

### Chain F — Merge pipeline correctness (from 2026-04-16 03:47 session)
**Goal:** Ensure every accepted evolution mutation reaches `output/` before the subgoal is declared FULFILLED. The 03:47 session showed 7/9 cycles with `merged=false` — only the initial Book files ever reached output despite accepted mutations for Members and Loans.

```
T1-CT  (meta.json double-write fix)  →  T1-CU  (merge-before-fulfillment gate)  →  T1-CV  (root auto-completion guard)
                                                                                     ↑
T1-CS  (EVOLUTION_NOOP output-term guard)  ──────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**T1-CT first (prerequisite for T1-CU):** The mutation `meta.json` must accurately reflect the subgoalId and merged state of the mutation itself, not the post-promotion cycle context. Without this, `FileVariantCollector` cannot find orphaned variants even if we make merge run before fulfillment.

**T1-CU second (the core fix):** Score-based closure must not fire until merge has occurred. This ensures the evolve→evaluate→merge→complete sequence is maintained. The merge-gate is the single change that breaks the "accepted but never merged" pattern.

**T1-CS in parallel (independent):** The `previousOutput` surface-term guard prevents false EVOLUTION_NOOP promotions (a different pathway to false FULFILLED). Can be implemented in parallel with T1-CT/CU.

**T1-CV last (depends on T1-CU):** Root auto-completion should not fire when replacement children under GIVEN_UP parents have no merges. Depends on T1-CU ensuring `mergeCount` is accurately incremented.

**T2-CG (independent, lower priority):** Signal cap prevents ROLE_EVOLUTION from drowning other priorities. Quality improvement; no functional dependency on the others.

---

### Chain G — Run 20260422_2 follow-ups
**Goal:** Close the correctness and steering gaps surfaced by the run 20260422_2 vs 20260422 comparison (analysis-cascade false GIVEN_UP, `new ServiceClass()` shadow state, jakarta↔javax merge regression, no compile ground truth).

**Original plan (2026-04-22):**

```
T1-CZ  →  T2-BZ Layer D  →  T4-AN-1  ┐
                                      ├─→  resume T3-BR / T3-BO audit / T3-BQ / T3-G1/G2
T2-CI  ──────────────────────────────┘
```

**Status update (2026-05-02):**
- **T1-CZ ✅ done.**
- **T2-BZ Layer D parked [>].** The Spring-stereotype `new ServiceClass()` regex was judged too narrow — it fires on one toycase pattern and misses the same bug expressed in any other DI framework or naming convention. The principled fix for the same class of failures is T4-AN-1 (the compiler will reject a broken `new BookService()` call when the constructor doesn't match), so investing in the regex first is wasted effort.
- **T2-CI downgraded.** Mostly subsumed by T4-AN-1: a jakarta↔javax import flip will fail compilation, and the compile-gate heuristic catches it with stronger evidence than an invariant snapshot. Residual value (catches the regression *before* merge rather than *after*) is small — keep the ticket but defer until after T4-AN-1 lands and re-evaluate.
- **T2-CM / T2-CN partially residual.** T4-AN-1 catches dangling references but **not** (a) orphan types declared without consumers — compile fine; (b) passive-only types — registered via `@ExceptionHandler(X.class)` but never thrown, compile fine; (c) annotation-key collisions (T2-CN) — two `@ExceptionHandler` advices for the same exception, compile fine, only break at runtime. These two heuristics retain real value even after T4-AN-1.
- **T2-CO mostly subsumed by T4-AN-2.** Spec-literal presence (does the output actually return 404?) is largely a runtime-test concern — keep the ticket but defer until after T4-AN-2.

**Revised order (2026-05-02):**

```
T3-BR ✅  →  T4-AN-1 ✅  →  T2-CM  →  T2-CN  →  (re-evaluate T2-CI / T2-CO / T5-J / T2-CR)
```

- **T3-BR first:** ✅ shipped 2026-05-02. Small, independent, fixes a real correctness gap (subgoals stall when fingerprint repeats but criteria are unbanked — e.g. after T3-BE analysis injects new criteria mid-subgoal). The `allCriteriaDefinedAndBanked` helper from T3-BL already exists; the fix replaces a boolean with a conjunction. No overlap with anything downstream.
- **T4-AN-1 second:** ✅ shipped 2026-05-02. New `BuildExecutor` runs `mvn compile` (configurable) post-merge; `BuildResultHeuristic` turns COMPILE_ERROR into REJECTED at CRITICAL; `EvaluationProtocolContract` surfaces the stderr tail in a `--- BUILD RESULT ---` block. Off by default via `autobuilder.build.enabled=false`. Replaces approximation with measurement.
- **T2-CM / T2-CN third:** the residue T4-AN-1 doesn't cover (orphan types, passive-only types, registration collisions). T2-CM also pairs with T3-BS (output-structure summary in evolution prompt) — same `OutputStructureAnalyzer` feeds both.
- **T5-J / T2-CI / T2-CO fourth (re-evaluate):** T5-J is cosmetic (phantom `mutation_scored` event). T2-CI and T2-CO are largely subsumed; revisit only if real gaps remain post-T4-AN-1/AN-2.
- **T2-CR fifth (gated on observation):** criteria-preserving refinement when REPLAN fires from T3-BR's `evaluator-repeat-cap`. Ship only after a live run confirms the cap actually fires — most stagnating subgoals are caught earlier by T2-BL / T1-CF / exhaustion machinery, so the redundancy concern may be theoretical until proven otherwise.
- T4-AN-2 (test runner) and the broader T4 agenda (T4-J / T4-AI / T4-AL) keep their existing priority — no evidence from these runs reorders them.

---

### Chain H — Build-First Discipline (from 2026-05-05 architecture review)

**Goal:** Compile correctness is a hard gate. While the build is red, no feature work proceeds; build-fix work runs in a tight, deterministic loop that delegates ecosystem-specific knowledge to the build tool itself and the LLM, not to hardcoded autobuilder logic.

**Motivation:** T4-AN-1 (post-merge compile gate) and T1-DM/DN/DP/DQ/DR (BUILD_FIX subgoal lifecycle) made the system *aware* of compile failures. They did not make compile correctness a first-class invariant. Two gaps remain:

1. **No hard execution gate.** After T1-DQ's cap (3 attempts) the heuristic abstains; combined with planner restructuring (T1-DP's recovered-from scenario) feature work can resume on broken code.
2. **Maven-specific knowledge baked in.** T2-CT's `BuildErrorClassifier` has 3 hardcoded `Kind` values; expanding it kind-by-kind doesn't generalise to other build tools and doesn't keep up with Maven's own message variety. Plugin/dependency compatibility resolution similarly should not live in autobuilder.

**Architecture decision (2026-05-05):** the only structural commitment is the `CONFIG_FIXABLE / SOURCE_FIXABLE / MIXED / UNKNOWN` partition. Everything else — the *kind* of error, the *artifact* at fault, the *coordinate* to upgrade to — is technology-specific and stays out of autobuilder's code: an LLM classifier (T4-AN-4) decides category + free-text fix hint, and the build tool's own diagnostics (T4-AN-5) supply remediation context. New build systems (Gradle, sbt, npm, Cargo) work on day one with yaml-only configuration.

**Order of work:** *(updated 2026-05-05 to insert T1-DW as a Chain H prereq and add T2-CW / T3-BT)*

```
Minimum viable subset (immediate wins, no new infrastructure):
T5-K  →  T1-DW  →  T1-DT  →  T2-CU  →  T1-DU
        (rollback) (gate)    (planner   (fitness
                              rule)      backstop)

Optional pre-empt (large bootstrap-quality win, parallel):
T3-BT (bootstrap-time build-system seeding) — removes BUILD_FIX from session start

New infrastructure (the adaptive layer):
T4-AN-4  →  T4-AN-7  →  T4-AN-5  →  T2-CW  →  T4-AN-6
(adaptive   (category   (build-tool  (consol-   (inner
 classifier) partition)  diagnostic)  idate)     loop)

Escalation safety nets (ship after T4-AN-6 lands):
T2-CV  (replan → analyze → pause; all three stages in one ticket)

Observability (parallel, anytime):
T5-N (build_fix_attempt events) — pairs with T5-M (cycle-end buildOutcome)
T5-O (drop Maven-specific stderr summariser) — after T4-AN-4
T5-P (build-cost telemetry) — pairs with T5-N for full attempt-level picture
```

- **T5-K first:** extract `BlockerSubgoalInjector` from `MergeAgent` so T1-DT can inject from outside the merge phase. Caller surface grows to 2 — the documented threshold for extraction. Pure refactor, no behaviour change.
- **T1-DW second (NEW prereq):** filesystem rollback when post-merge build fails. Without rollback, force-accepted broken `pom.xml` becomes the new on-disk baseline; each force-accept compounds the breakage. T1-DT's gate is meaningful only if rollback keeps state recoverable.
- **T1-DT third:** hard gate on feature subgoals while COMPILE_ERROR carries. The single-most-important behavioural change in the chain.
- **T2-CU fourth:** planner BUILD STATE block + `BUILD_FIRST` rule. Cheap, makes the planner honest while T1-DT keeps execution honest.
- **T1-DU fifth:** `BUILD_BROKEN` fitness signal. Backstop for marginal ranking cases.
- **T3-BT — parallel pre-empt:** if shipped early, removes BUILD_FIX from session start (the dominant trigger today). Doesn't replace Chain H — mid-session BUILD_FIX from a bad mutation still happens — just reduces frequency. Low coupling to other Chain H tickets.
- **T4-AN-4 sixth:** adaptive classifier replaces kind-expansion. Drops Maven-specific commitments from autobuilder code.
- **T4-AN-7 seventh (small):** category field on `Classification`, drop kind-to-category mapping. Trivial after T4-AN-4.
- **T4-AN-5 eighth:** build-tool diagnostic probe. Delegates compatibility resolution to the tool. Yaml-extensible to any ecosystem.
- **T2-CW ninth (small):** consolidate the three hardcoded build-config file lists into `BuildToolProbe.configFilenames`. Bundles cleanly with T4-AN-5.
- **T4-AN-6 tenth:** intra-cycle inner retry loop. Costs ~1 LLM call per attempt vs ~6 for an outer cycle. Default off until validated.
- **T2-CV eleventh:** signal/graph-driven build-fix escalation (replan on `REPETITION_DETECTED`/`BUILD_BROKEN.age` → analyze when planner already acted but build still broken). T1-EB is the backstop halt; T2-CV's goal is to make T1-EB unnecessary. Now safe to ship because T1-DT prevents broken-build feature drift and T4-AN-5/6 give the analysis prompt enough context to be useful.
- **T5-N parallel:** observability for the chain. Should land before T4-AN-6 to keep the inner loop visible.
- **T5-O after T4-AN-4:** delete Maven-specific stderr summariser; thread classifier output through. Quality / dead-code removal.
- **T5-P pairs with T5-N:** per-attempt build cost; together with T5-N they give full attempt-level visibility for the chain.

---

### Chain I — Pre-evaluation compile ground truth (from 2026-05-09 04:14 session)
**Goal:** Give the LLM evaluator compile ground truth for the *proposed* mutation before any merge or rollback occurs, and escalate to the planner when compile errors repeat so the loop does not burn cycles on detectable repeating failures.

**Motivation:** T4-AN-1 (post-merge compile gate) catches compile errors after the filesystem is written and the LLM has already been called. The 2026-05-09 run showed 14 rollbacks in 58 cycles — LLM scored 0.85 ACCEPTED for code with `incompatible types: inference variable T` generics errors and missing `ErrorResponse` symbol errors. The evaluator can't detect these without a compiler. The result is a guaranteed rollback loop: evaluate → merge → compile fail → rollback → evaluate again. T2-DG complements T1-EC as the circuit breaker when pre-compile still doesn't prevent all rollbacks.

```
T1-EC  (pre-evaluation compile staging)  →  T2-DG  (rollback-recurrence escalation, safety net)
```

- **T1-EC first (primary fix):** Stage the mutation to output/, run `mvn compile`, feed stderr to the LLM evaluator as `--- COMPILE RESULT ---`. `BuildResultHeuristic` vetoes ACCEPTED on COMPILE_ERROR before any merge is attempted. Eliminates the merge-rollback waste for the majority of compile failures. Adds one `mvn compile` per evaluation cycle (~3–5s overhead).
- **T2-DG second (safety net):** When the same subgoal causes compile-error rollbacks in ≥2 consecutive cycles, emit `COMPILE_FIX_NEEDED` + `REPLAN_REQUESTED`. The planner runs next cycle with the exact compile error in its prompt and creates a targeted subgoal. Catches failures that T1-EC misses (e.g. compile passes in staging but fails in the full project context).
